## Eviction pressure

Allocated bytes (sum of st_blocks × 512) include objects, temporary files, metadata and directory blocks. Scans are not atomic; vanished files are ignored.
The configured cap accounts for blobs, not all these filesystem costs. nginx has no cap/evictor.

## Limits and replay

See ../README.md: loopback, shared machine, hot kernel page cache, different durability policies.
cachette is unmeasured unless explicitly present above; no comparative claim about it follows.

Final replay from the repository root on dev2 (after store/http merge):

```sh
sudo timeout 3600 python3 bench/run.py --work-dir /home/debian/cachette-bench --seconds 10
```
